### Market Context

### Ticker Notes
- **AMD** (+8.93%): AI demand driving targets to $375 (Susquehanna). Up 50% from March low ($196→$343). Earnings May 5 (est. $1.27 EPS, $9.84B rev). Insider sales noted. Momentum strong but approaching earnings binary.
- **NVDA** (+1.06%): FY2026 revenue $215.9B (+65% YoY). Blackwell Ultra ramping. Earnings May 20. Big-tech CapEx (Alphabet, Meta, Amazon, MSFT) all maintained/raised — positive inference demand signal. ML model targets $200.75 today.
- **PLTR** (-2.33%): Trading ~$139-143 (down 31% from $207 peak). AI sentiment bearish short-term. Q4 2025 beats: rev +70%, U.S. commercial +137%. Earnings early May. Stop at $129.47 — thesis intact, but weak action.

### Trade Ideas
1. **XOM or CVX (Energy)** — Oil at $108 (Strait of Hormuz risk premium), energy sector top YTD performer (+22-40%). Entry: wait for post-8:30 AM macro data clarity. Stop: -10% trail. Target: 15-20% over 4-6 weeks. R:R: ~1.5-2:1. Conviction: 6/10 — sector alignment strong but entering after a massive move; oil +90% from Jan lows creates mean-reversion risk. ❌ Below 8/10 threshold — DO NOT BUY.
2. **AMD add (Tech)** — Earnings May 5 catalyst with $9.84B rev consensus. Could add pre-earnings for continued momentum. Conviction: 5/10 — buying into earnings binary in underperforming sector. ❌ Below threshold.
3. **HOLD cash** — Triple macro release (GDP + PCE + ECI) at 8:30 creates binary volatility. Await prints before any action; data could whipsaw positions in either direction.

### Risk Factors
- Triple 8:30 AM data release (GDP, PCE, ECI) — if GDP weak + PCE hot, stagflation fear sell-off
- VIX rising (18.81, +5.5% yesterday) — elevated and moving up ahead of data

- PLTR -2.33% below entry with weak price action; stop at $129.47 (~7.6% below current)
- Oil parabolic move from $57→$108 in 3 months — reversal risk if tension de-escalates
- FOMC transition (Powell exit) adds policy uncertainty

### Decision
**HOLD** — No new buys today.
- Existing positions are within stops; no manual exits warranted.
- No trade idea clears the 8/10 conviction threshold.
- Triple macro data at 8:30 AM argues strongly for patience.
- Deployed at 59.1% — below target, but capital preservation ahead of binary events (earnings + GDP) is correct.
- Re-evaluate energy add (XOM/CVX) Friday once GDP/PCE and early earnings digest.
